UIData_ShowProblem_eZys=VIP Advanced Data
UIData_ShowProblem_SM0y=Total hands: The hands player used to play in ALLIN.\n\nTotal games: The games player used to play in ALLIN.\n\nVPIP: The ratio player ever raises or calls before flop in total hands.\n\nWins: The ratio player wins after raising or calling before flop in total hands.\n\nAvg result: The average achievement of all games played.\n\nAvg buy-in: The average buy-in of all games played.
UIData_ShowProblem_3xqp=VPIP : VPIP suit le pourcentage de mains dans lesquelles un joueur en particulier met volontairement de l'argent dans le pré-flop de pot. Le VPIP augmente lorsqu'un joueur peut se plier, mais au lieu de cela engage de l'argent dans le pot preflop. Cela comprend boiter (simplement appeler les grands aveugles), appeler et élever. \n\n Pre-Flop Raise (PFR) : PFR suit le pourcentage de mains dans lesquelles un joueur particulier fait une augmentation de préflop lorsqu'il a l'occasion de plier ou d'appeler à la place.(3Bet) : Un 3Bet est le troisième pari dans une séquence de poker, dans lequel un joueur relance après l'augmentation initiale de pré-flop, ou pari de 2. Pari de Continuation (CBet) : Lorsqu’un joueur lance ou relève un pari au tour précédent, il lance un autre pari actif au tour actuel\n\n Facteur d'Agression (AF) : Plus cette valeur est élevée, plus le joueur est agressif, et plus il est enclin à parier/relever. Il est calculé comme {(nombre de paris + nombre de relances)/nombre d'appels}/50 (diviser par 50 revient à convertir en pourcentage, ce qui scelle la valeur à 100%, c'est-à-dire la valeur maximale d'origine de 50) \n\n Aller à l'épreuve de force (WTSD) : Le taux d'épreuve de force est le pourcentage d'un joueur qui voit un cercle de flop et joue une épreuve de force. Le calcul est le nombre de mains dans le showdown/le nombre de mains dans le flop. \n\nTaux de victoire de All-in (All-in Wins) : Une statistique du taux de victoire de main d'un joueur après avoir gagné tout-en-un. Une valeur plus élevée signifie que le joueur chancelle rarement et gagne essentiellement lors du all-in. \n\nTaux de victoires des mises en pot volontaires (VPIP Wins) : Le ratio de joueurs gagnant dans les mains qui sont mises en pot.

UITexasRule_title113=Minimum time on table
UITexasRule_detail3=When the table admin turns on the minimum playing time, the player must at least play in the game for the minimum time, otherwise the system will implement the hosting mode of keeping in blind to play for the player.
UITexasRule_detail4=1. Activate insurance when all players do All-in
UITexasRule_detail5=2. Currently there is a  purchase proceeding by the player with the highest card type. If there are multiple pots, the player who leads the largest pot will buy it, and only the largest pot can be purchased
UITexasRule_detail6=3. The insurance is not activated when there are more than 3 players in the pot(Insurance can be bought when there are 2 or 3 players).
UITexasRule_detail7=4. It’s impossible to purchase insurance for the three cards on the flop, the insured amount on the turn shall not exceed 0.25*pot, and the insured amount for river cards shall not exceed 0.5*pot
UITexasRule_detail8=5. If the turn card insurance is purchased, the system will automatically purchase a small amount of insurance for the river card, and the insurance investment of the back turn card
UITexasRule_detail9=5. If the turn card insurance purchase is not successful, the river card must purchase all outs, and the Compensate of the river card must be greater than the insured amount of the turn card
UITexasRule_detail10=6. If the player only buys part of the replacement card, the system will automatically purchase the remaining replacement card to avoid the risk of the player being BB, and bring back the player's insurance amount
UITexasRule_detail11=7.  Insurance data displayed in the [Real-time Achievement] [Hands record] and [Achievement details] in the Career
UITexasRule_detail12=8. When the Texas, Omaha outs exceeds 30 cards and  short deck outs exceeds 19 cards, the insurance is not activated
UITexasRule_detail13=9. When most of the players do all-in, and appear more than one pot, the player with the leading hand can only purchase the outs insurance in the maximum pot that he has participated in.
UITexasRule_title114=Operations
UITexasRule_detail15=1. Guaranteed: Compensate = player investment
UITexasRule_detail16=2. Full pot: equal returns regardless of whether the insurance is met or not

UITexasRule_title116=Texas Introduction and Rules
UITexasRule_title117=How to play
UITexasRule_title118=Short Deck Rules
UITexasRule_detail19=1. There are four rounds in Texas Hold'em; \n\n2. Before the flop: each person will be dealt with 2 cards and then the first round; \n\n3. The flop: after the third common card is dealt, the first round will be played Second round;\n\n4.Turn: the third round after the 4th common card is dealt;\n\n5.River: the fourth round after the 5th common card is dealt ;\n\n6. After the game is over, all remaining players will compare the cards, and the biggest one wins the pot. \n\nComparison of cards: Royal Flush>Straight Flush>Four of a Kind>Full House>Flush>Straight>Three of a Kind>Two Pairs>One Pair>High Card\nThe cards are from big to small: A, K, Q, J ,10,9,8,7,6,5,4,3,2 each suit regardless of size
UITexasRule_detail20=Texas Hold'em, which began in the early 20th century in Texas, is the most popular public poker game in Europe and America with a history of hundreds of years. Texas Hold'em is a poker game favored by chess and card lovers all over the world because of its easy to learn and difficult to learn.
UITexasRule_detail21=1. Omaha is a variant of Texas Hold Poker, with the same basic rules as Texas Hold Poker with only minor differences \n\n2. Omaha gives each player five or six hands and Texas Hold two; \n\n3. Omaha must use two of five or six cards to form the best one hand with three cards; \n\n4. Omaha is divided into Pool Limits and No Limits Omaha, We use the bottom pool limit Omaha;\n\n5. Bottom pool limit Omaha: The player can not exceed 1 times the bottom pool\nThe calculation formula: The upper limit = flat amount + flat amount + current bottom pool, that is, the player needs to follow the flat amount of bets before following the flat double bottom pool.
UITexasRule_detail22=AOF rule: in the pre-flop stage, only ALLIN or FOLD can be selected,\n after all players have acted, the ALLIN players will compete cards; \n\n Hand display restriction: After dealing a card, the player does not show his hand immediately, and only sees his hand when it is his turn to act.
UITexasRule_detail23=1. Mandatory Ante totally have four rounds of game; \n\n2. Pre- flop: The first round of game occurs after 2 cards are dealt to each player; \n\n3. Flop: the second round of game after dealing 3 community cards; \n\n4. Turn round: the third round of game after the fifth community card is dealt; \n\ N5. River round: the fourth round of game after the fifth community card is dealt; \n\n6. After the end of the game, all the remaining players compare cards, and the largest player wins the bottom pool. \ N \ N brand comparison: Royal Flush>Straight Flush>Four of a Kind>Full House>Flush>Straight>Three of a Kind>Two Pairs>One Pair>High Card \ n \ n CARDS from big to small is: A.K.Q.J. 1.0.9.8.7.6.5.4.3.2 each design and color, big or small \ n just at the time of creating table, before the blind selection range is bigger than ordinary Texas table
UITexasRule_detail24=1. On the basis of the original standard Texas/Omaha, the 16 cards "2-5" are removed and the 9*4=36 cards "6-A" are used, and the number of players is limited to 4~6; \n\n2. At the beginning of the game, each player will be dealt 2 "bottom cards" and the table will be dealt 3, 1 and 1 (5 cards in total) public cards in three successive rounds, after four rounds of "raise", "call" and "fold" After four rounds of "raise", "call" and "fold", if there are at least two players who have not folded, the player enters the "showdown" stage and selects the largest 5-card combination of his or her 2 bottom cards and 5 public cards, and divides the winner according to the rules of hand size. The winner takes all UC in the "bottom pool"; \n\n3. The size of the cards are: Royal Flush > Straight Flush > Four of a Kind > Flush > Full House > Three of a Kind > Straight > Two Pairs > One Pair > High Card

UIData_TerminolD6qL3kUc=VIP Data
UIData_TerminolbazUAGg6=Pre-Flop Raise（PFR）:  PFR tracks the percentage of hands in which a particular player makes a preflop raise when having the opportunity to fold or call instead.Calculated as the number of Pre-Flop Raise/the number of Pre-Flop bet\n\n3Bet: A 3-bet is the third bet in a poker sequence, in which a player re-raises after the initial pre-flop raise, or 2-bet.Calculated as the number of 3Bet/the number of Pre-Flop bet\n\nContinuation Bet（CBet）: When a player initiates or raises a bet in the previous round, then initiates another active bet in the current round\n\nAggression factor (AF): The higher this value is, the more aggressive the player is, and the more inclined to bet/raise. It is calculated as {(number of bets + number of raises)/number of calls}/50 (dividing by 50 is to convert to a percentage, which seals the value at 100%, i.e. the original maximum value of 50)  \nFormula is (bet + raise) / call / 50 \n\nWTSD: The showdown rate is the percentage of a player who sees a flop circle and plays a showdown. The calculation is the number of hands in the showdown/the number of hands in the flop.\n\nWin rate of All-in (All-in Wins): A statistic of a player's hand win rate after going all-in. A higher value means that the player rarely staggers and basically wins when going all-in.

UIMTT_Howtoplay_rules=Game rules
UIMTT_Howtoplay_rulesc=MTT [Multi Table Tournament] means a tournament played on multiple tables at the same time. The tables will be closed as players are eliminated, and will be reduced to one table going into the final until one person has stacks left in the tournament. The winner is determined and the tournament ends.
UIMTT_Howtoplay_scoreboard=Withdraw Stacks
UIMTT_Howtoplay_scoreboardc=During the tournament, all players will automatically bring in the same fixed number of stack, players only need to sign up for the tournament and cannot restock the stack at their own will during the tournament
UIMTT_Howtoplay_blind=Blind
UIMTT_Howtoplay_blindc=The blinds structure of the match is different from the fixed blinds of the standard round. There will be an initial blind, and as the game progresses, the blind level will rise regularly, and the game rhythm will become faster and faster.
UIMTT_Howtoplay_Zmtime=Blind Interval
UIMTT_Howtoplay_Zmtimec=The blind increase time is set by the game organizer. Every other blind increase time, the blinds will increase by one level. The blind rise time can control the rhythm of the game. After the game starts, you can’t love the blind rise time
UIMTT_Howtoplay_refactor=Rebuy
UIMTT_Howtoplay_refactorc=When a player is eliminated from the game, he can rebuy and resurrect. There is a limit on the number of times of rebuy, which is set by the competition organizer. Rebuy is only allowed during the registration stage. Rebuy is not allowed during the prohibitation of the registration period and the final stage.
UIMTT_Howtoplay_Intermission=Intermission
UIMTT_Howtoplay_Intermissionc=After opening the intermission, the game will automatically be suspended every 55 minutes and enter a 3-minute rest.
UIMTT_Howtoplay_czhz=Redistributing Table

If there are multiple pots, the player who leads the largest pot will buy it, and only the largest pot can be purchased\n\n3. Insurance is only sold for all-in situations with 3 players in the pot or less.\n\n4.Three cards on the flop cannot be insured, the insured amount on the turn shall not exceed 0.25*pot, and the insured amount for river cards shall not exceed 0.5*pot\n\n5. If the turn card purchase insurance is not successful, the river card must purchase all outs, and the payout of the river card must be greater than the insured amount of the turn card\n\n6. If the player only buys part of the replacement card, the system will automatically purchase the remaining replacement card to avoid the risk of the player being BB and bring back the player's insurance amount\n\n7.Insurance data displayed in the [Real-time Achievement] [Approval of the previous game] and [Details of the game] in the career\n\n8.Texas,Omaha, when the outs exceed 30, the insurance is not activated

UITexasRule_Introduce=<b><size=50>Introduction</size></b>\nTexas Hold'em, which began in the early 20th century in Texas, is the most popular public poker game in Europe and America with a history of hundreds of years. Texas Hold'em is a poker game favored by chess and card lovers all over the world.\n\n<b><size=50>Gameplay</size></b>\n1.There are four rounds in Texas Hold'em;\n2.Pre-flop: each player will be dealt with 2 cards and then start the first round;\n3.Flop: after the three community cards are dealt, the remaining players from the first round will start the second round;\n4.Turn: the third round after the 4th community card is dealt;\n5.River: the fourth round after the 5th community card is dealt;\n6.After the game is over, all remaining players must show their cards and the player with the highest hand wins the pot.\n\n<b><size=50>Ranking of poker hand:</size></b>\nRoyal Flush>Straight Flush>Four of a kind>Full House>Flush>Straight>Three of a kind>Two Pairs>One Pair>High Card\nRanking of the card: \nA>K>O>J>10>9>8>7>6>5>4>3>2, regardless of suit\n\n
UIPlO4Rule_Introduce=<b><size=50>Introduction</size></b>\nOmaha is very similar to Texas Hold’em with one game-changing factor. Instead of two hand cards, each player is dealt four to six cards. In Omaha, players must make the best possible hand with a combination that two from their hand cards and three from the community cards.\n\n<b><size=50>PLO4</size></b>\nIn Pot Limit Omaha (the most popular form of Omaha Poker), the maximum a player can put stacks is the same amount as in the pot. “4” means four cards will be dealt to each player.\n\n<b><size=50>Gameplay</size></b>\n1. There are four rounds in Omaha;\n2.Pre-flop: each player will be dealt with 4 cards and then start the first round;\n3.Flop: After the three community cards are dealt, the remaining players from the first round will start the second round;\n4.Turn the third round after the 4th community card is dealt;\n5.River: the fourth round after the 5th community card is dealt;\n6.After the game is over, all remaining players must show their cards and the player with the highest hand wins the pot.\n\n<b><size=50>Ranking of poker hand:</size></b>\nRoyal Flush>Straight Flush>Four of a kind>Full House>Flush>Straight>Three of a kind>Two Pairs>One Pair>High Card\nRanking of the card: \nA>K>O>J>10>9>8>7>6>5>4>3>2, regardless of suit\n\n

In Omaha, players must make the best possible hand with a combination that two from their hand cards and three from the community cards.\n\n<b><size=50>PLO5</size></b>\nIn Pot Limit Omaha (most popular form of Omaha Poker), the maximum a player can put UC is as the same amount as in the pot. “5” means five cards will be dealt to each player.\n\n<b><size=50>Gameplay</size></b>\n1.There are five rounds in Omaha;\n2.Pre-flop: each player will be dealt with 5 cards and then start the first round;\n3.Flop: after the three community cards are dealt, the remaining players from the first round will start the second round;\n4.Turn: the third round after the 4th community card is dealt;\n5.River: the fourth round after the 5th community card is dealt;\n6.After the game is over, all remaining players must show their cards and the player with the highest hand wins the pot.\n\n<b><size=50>Ranking of poker hand:</size></b>\nRoyal Flush>Straight Flush>Four of a kind>Full House>Flush>Straight>Three of a kind>Two Pairs>One Pair>High Card\nRanking of the card: \nA>K>O>J>10>9>8>7>6>5>4>3>2, regardless of suit\n\n

UI6Plus_Introduce=<b><size=46>6+ Hold'em</size></b>\n6+ Hold'em is a popular “short deck” poker gameplay that mostly follows the same rules with Texas Hold’em, but with a few exciting differences:\n1.All cards lower than 6 are removed from the deck;\n2.Everyone put UC and only the button blind - known as the “button blind”;\n3.Flush beats Full-house and A, 6, 7, 8, 9 is classified as lowest straight\nAvailable exclusively at NEW POKER, 6+ Hold’em gives you the chance to play an action-packed gameplay loved by high-stakes players around the world.\n\n

UIHappyShop_RulerDetail=<P><T>NEW POKER has launched a "Super 1" event to thank our VIP players for their continued support. Every VIP player of NEW POKER will not only enjoy the special privileges such as the exclusive profile picture frame in the game, but also get a coupon to participate in the special "Super 1" reward event. <P><H>1. Event introduction <P><T>1) "Super 1" prepares a variety of rich gifts for VIP players waiting for everyone to draw! <P><T>2) "Super 1" divides each gift into several equal parts, and one coupon can be used to draw the selected gift once. When the players draw 1 time, they will get a code. When the number of all parts of the gift has been drawn, the system will calculate a "lucky code" according to the rules of the event, and the owner of this "lucky code" can get this gift! <P><H>2. Rules of participation <P><T>1) Each player can purchase a VIP service through "My Shop" in NEW POKER and receive a complimentary event coupon to participate in "Super 1". Each day of VIP service purchase will receive one complimentary event coupon. <P><T>2) Each player can earn an event coupon by completing "Super 1 Daily Missions" in NEW POKER as well. <P><H>3. Rules of reward<P><T>1)When the last number of the gift is allocated, the system will extract the participation TIME of the last 50 participating users of the gift (if the number is less than 50, the maximum number will be taken). <P><T>2) Sum the values of the time to obtain the "value A" (each TIME is combined in the order of hour, minute, second and millisecond, for example, 20:15:23.345 is 201523345). <P><T>3) Dividing the "value A" by the total numbers of the gift gives a remainder. Add this remainder to 1000001 will be the final "lucky code". The player with the "lucky code" wins the gift directly. <P><H>4.Prize rules<P><T>1) For physical prizes, the winner is required to collect the items within 3 months after winning it, and provide a valid mailing address according to the platform tips. In case of no return for more than 3 months the platform will take as the winning user is giving up the prize.<P><T>2)  For physical prizes, after registering a valid mailing address, all of them require 60 working days for shipping.<P><T>3)  Any physical prize can be exchanged for UC with a service tax off the value of the prize, according to the platform rules.<P><T>4)  If the prize is a NEW POKER virtual prop, the platform will directly send it to the winning player's wallet, please check it for yourself.<P><H>5. Rule of cancellation <P><T>1) Once the player uses the coupon to participate in the gift draw, will not be able to cancel or return. <P><T>2) If a single gift in the "Super 1" event does not meet the required total number of times within a certain period, or there are some uncontrollable factors, NEW POKER has the right to cancel the gift in the middle of the process, and the original number of coupons will be returned to the user. <P><H>6. Declaration of rights<P><T>1) After-sales services of all gifts of "Super 1" belong to the gift brand and have nothing to do with the platform. <P><T>2) The duration of "Super 1" is determined by the NEW POKER Game platform, and NEW POKER may change the timing, rewards, and everything about this event at any time. The decision of the platform is final and shall not be challenged or appealed.\n

In the same order as before, deal each board turn (hidden cards), then deal each board river (hidden cards).\n6, Showdown the 1st board hand and all public cards(show part of the result).\n7, Showdown the 2nd board hand and all public cards (show partial results).\n8, Showdown the 3rd board hand and all public cards (show part of the result). \n9,  Summarize results, calculate bonus, and proceed to next round. \n\nToroFlops Special Rules: \n1, When a player gets a full house in any board, which is composed of 3 cards J/Q/K/A, the next round will enter Fantasy.\n2, Fantasy player has a clear visual incentive. \n3, Fantasy players have 12 cards and no grouping is required. Non-fantasy players need to be group as usual. \n4, After the 3 board flop public cards are dealt, fantasy players directly choose 6 of the 12 cards group as 2/2/2 to match each board. Non-Fantasy players same as regularl rules. \n5, Follow regular rules and showdown \n6, Summarize results and proceed to next round.

Mahjong_RuleTitle1=Basic Gameplay
Mahjong_RuleContent1_1=Sichuan Mahjong is played with 108 tiles that do not contain character tiles (i.e. no East, South, West, North, Red, Green, or White Dragon), and only includes 36 tiles of each of the three color sequences: 1-9 Character, 1-9 Bamboo, and 1-9 Bamboo.
Mahjong_RuleContent1_2=Roll the dice to determine the Dealer, followed by the player who has won, if multiple players declaring a win on a single discard, the discarder will be the dealer, if draw then dealer keep same.
Mahjong_RuleContent1_3=Each player starts with 13 cards, the dealer has 14 cards.
Mahjong_RuleContent1_4=Pong and kong are allowed, but no chow is allowed
Mahjong_RuleTitle2=Mahjong winning rules
Mahjong_RuleContent2_1=Winning by picking up a discard, winning by claiming a tile to complete a set when someone declares a Kong are allowed, and supporting multiple winners on a single discard. Must lack a suit. In Blood Fight, players must 'lack one suit,' meaning they cannot have any tiles of a particular suit, ensuring that when a player wins, they have only two suits remaining in their hand.
Mahjong_RuleContent2_2=In the "Blood Fight" mode, after one player wins, the remaining players continue the game until either all three players win or the game ends in a draw. The winner receives points from the player who discarded the winning tile. If the winner drew the winning tile themselves, all other three players pay. Players who have already won do not participate in the subsequent rounds and can freely leave the game.
Mahjong_RuleTitle3=Exchange Three Tiles
Mahjong_RuleContent3_1=After dealing the initial hand, each player selects three tiles of the same suit and exchanges them randomly with another player.
Mahjong_RuleContent3_2=Exchange Three Tiles" is one of the gameplay options. Players can choose to activate or deactivate this mode.
Mahjong_RuleContent3_3=If "Exchange Three Tiles" is triggered, players skip the exchange if they already have a good hand. The condition for triggering this is when two of the three suits have fewer than three tiles each.
Mahjong_RuleTitle4=Lack of One Suit
Mahjong_RuleContent4_1=After the initial deal, players must choose and discard tiles of one suit (Dots, Bamboo, or Characters), which they will not use for the entire game. They will only use tiles from the remaining two suits to form their winning hand.
Mahjong_RuleContent4_2=This choice can be adjusted freely before winning or before drawing the last tile, unlike "Fixed Lack."
Mahjong_RuleTitle5=Fixed Lack
Mahjong_RuleContent5_1=After dealing the initial hand, selecting one suit to discard becomes 'Fixed Lack.' Once confirmed, this choice cannot be changed and tiles of the fixed lack suit must be discarded if drawn.
Mahjong_RuleContent5_2=The difference between this rule and "Lack of One Suit" is that once confirmed, the choice cannot be changed.
Mahjong_RuleContent5_3=The suit designated as "Fixed Lack" renders its tiles inactive; they cannot be used for winning, Pong, or Kong.
Mahjong_RuleTitle6=Kong Rules
Mahjong_RuleContent6_1=Kong determines the immediate outcome of the game and results in immediate settlement.
Mahjong_RuleContent6_2=Scoring a Wind,' which includes claiming a tile to complete a Kong or declaring a Kong with a tile just drawn, results in the player who discarded the tile paying double the base points.
Mahjong_RuleContent6_3="Supplementary Kong", Charge other players 1x the base stake.
Mahjong_RuleContent6_4=Raining Down,' which refers to declaring a concealed Kong, results in other players paying double the base points.
Mahjong_RuleTitle7=End of Game
Mahjong_RuleContent7_1=When all hands are drawn, players who are not ready or are "flower pigs" will receive additional penalties, which can be toggled when creating a room.
Mahjong_RuleTitle8=Checking for High Hands
Mahjong_RuleContent8_1=Checking for High Hands', Players without ready hand must compensate the player who with ready hand but not yet won with the maximum potential multiplier (excluding self-drawn multipliers).
Mahjong_RuleTitle9=Checking for Pig Hands
Mahjong_RuleContent9_1=Checking for Pig Hands', When all tiles are drawn, the player with tiles from all three suits is considered to have a Pig Hand. The Pig Hand pays 16 times the base points to players without ready hand and players who have declared a win. It pays the multiplier from 'Checking for High Hands' plus 16 times the base points to players with ready hand but not yet won. If both 'Checking for Pig Hands' and 'Checking for High Hands' occur together, only the penalty from 'Checking for Pig Hands' is applied.
Mahjong_RuleTitle10=Points Rules
Mahjong_RuleContent10_1=Fan calculation: Fan Type 1 × Fan Type 2 × ... × Base Stake\nBase Stake: The base score unit selected by the room creator. 1 base stake equals 1 chip
Mahjong_RuleTitle11=Scoring Type:
Mahjong_RuleTitle12=Doubles
Mahjong_RuleContent12_1=Plain Hand (Basic Win)": Four sets of three identical tiles (kans) plus a pair of identical tiles (jantou). For example, 23445677 in Bamboo or 345789 in Characters.
Mahjong_RuleTitle13=Double
Mahjong_RuleContent13_1=【Pure Pairs Hand (All Pairs, Pure Pairs)】, except one pair, the remaining tiles consist of four sets of three identical tiles, For example, 111222444 in Characters and 33399 in Bamboo.
Mahjong_RuleTitle14=Four times
Mahjong_RuleContent14_1=【Pure Suit】, All tiles in the winning hand are from a single suit. For example, 11 333 222 456 999 in Bamboo.
Mahjong_RuleContent14_2=【Golden Hook】, When declaring a win, all other tiles in the hand have been used in Pongs or Kongs, leaving only one tile remaining to win, not counting Pure Pairs.
Mahjong_RuleContent14_3=【Seven Pairs】The player's hand consists entirely of pairs, with no melds or kongs, e.g., 22 44 55 77 99 Characters, 11 99 Dots
Mahjong_RuleContent14_4=【Honors and Terminals】, Each set (triplet or sequence) used to win must contain honor tiles or terminal tiles (ones or nines).
Mahjong_RuleTitle15=Eight Times
Mahjong_RuleContent15_1=【Pure Pairs Pure Suit (Pure Pairs Pong)】, The player's hand consists of Pure Pairs in a single suit.
Mahjong_RuleContent15_2=【Eyes Pairs (Eyes Pongs)】, All sets in the hand consist of Pongs of 2s, 5s, and 8s.
Mahjong_RuleContent15_3=【Dragon Seven Pairs】, The player's hand consists of concealed seven pairs with no Pongs or Kongs, and there are four identical tiles, called Dragon Seven Pairs rather than Seven Pairs. For example, 11222244 in Bamboo or 557799 in Characters.
Mahjong_RuleTitle16=Sixteen Times
Mahjong_RuleContent16_1=【Pure Seven Pairs】, The player's hand consists of seven pairs, all from a single suit. For example, 11 33 44 66 7 788 99 in Bamboo.
Mahjong_RuleContent16_2=【Eye Seven Pairs】, All pairs in the hand consist of 2s, 5s, and 8s.
Mahjong_RuleContent16_3=【Pure Golden Hook】, A hand that combines Pure Suit and Golden Hook.
Mahjong_RuleTitle17=Thirty-two times
Mahjong_RuleContent17_1=【Pure Dragon Seven Pairs】, The player's hand consists of concealed seven pairs, all from a single suit, known as Pure Dragon Seven Pairs. For example, 11 22 4444 55 77 99 in Characters.
Mahjong_RuleContent17_2=【Heaven Hand】, The dealer wins without making any Pongs or Kongs after exchanging tiles and without discarding any tiles.
Mahjong_RuleContent17_3=【Earthly Hand】, A non-dealer wins without making any Pongs or Kongs after exchanging tiles and without discarding any tiles.
Mahjong_RuleTitle18=Additional calculation
Mahjong_RuleContent18_1=’Supplemental tile of melding quad': Doubles the points. Winning with a self-drawn tile after declaring a Kong (drawing a tile to complete a Kong) and then winning. \n\n'Winning on a Kong': Doubles the points. Occurs when a player declares a Kong, draws a tile to replace the one used in the Kong, and then another player wins by taking the discarded tile, which completes their hand. \n\n'Robbing a Kong': Doubles the points. Happens when a player wins by taking the tile used to complete another player's Kong. \n\n'Root': Doubles the points. Sets of four identical tiles in hand count as one root. Each root doubles the points when winning. \n\n'Self-Drawn': Doubles the points. The tile drawn by the player themselves for winning doubles the points. \n\n'Winning on the Last Draw': Doubles the points. Winning by drawing the last tile from the wall. \n\n'Plain Hand': Single points. Directly multiplies the base points by 1.

Each player (in current round of squid game) will get a squid mark after winning the pot for the first time, which will be displayed on the avatar on the table. A draw will not generate a squid mark. \n3. When a player gets the "squid mark" and there is only one player left without the "squid mark", this round of squid game ends, and the last player needs to pay penalty points to other players. \n4. If the player does not have enough balance to pay the penalty amount, the corresponding amount will be deducted from the deposit, and the player will be in a standing state. The player can bring in and sit down again\n5. This round of squid game ends, all states are reset, and a new round is ready. Exiting the squid mode midway will be regarded as surrender and compensation will be required. Please be careful. \n\n<size=46>Exit and Penalty Rules</size>\n\n1. Players who leave actively, leave passively, or temporarily disconnect are all considered "leaving state". \n2. If you are not in the squid mode, you will leave the table normally and entire deposit will be directly return when you leave; otherwise, you will need to deduct the penalty amount of the number of people who have obtained the "squid mark + 1" in the leaving state. \n3. Players who are in the "leave state" and have a "squid mark" will leave the table after settlement, and the deposit will be returned without additional deductions. \n 4. Players who are in the "leave state" and do not have a "squid mark" will pay the penalty amount to each player who has a "squid mark" and then leave the table after settlement. If the amount is not enough, it will be deducted from the deposit. \n5. Player can continue and keep the same status If reconnects successfully within the countdown, and the squid game settlement is not triggered during the period. \n6. The penalty amount for quitting midway will be put into the squid pool and will be divided equally among the players who have the squid mark after the end of this round of squid.

Advantages & Ideal Players\n- Faster pace, exciting multiplier: Critical Strike mode triggers forced raises at set intervals, increasing game intensity and allowing players to experience greater volatility and earning potential in shorter timeframes.\n- Ideal for: Offensive players who enjoy fast-paced, high-risk high-reward games. Execution\n- Mandatory: Players on table during Critical round must post the blind. UISquidNewRulerDetail=<size=52>Squid Game Rules</size>\n\nSquid Game (Stand Up Game) is an elimination-style mode derived from Texas Hold’em. Compared with traditional Hold’em, it adds more competitive elements and makes the game more thrilling.\n\n<size=52>Winning & Losing</size>\n\n• Winner: Each time a player wins the pot, they receive one squid. Anyone holding a squid is a winner. (If the pot is split, no squid is awarded; with multiple pots, the main pot winner receives the squid.)\n\n• Loser: Players who do not obtain a squid are losers and will be penalized.\n\n<size=52>Rewards & Penalties</size>\n\n• Losers must pay a squid bonus from their balance; if insufficient, it is taken from the deposit. The amount depends on the winners’ number of squids and the squid value.\n\n
UISquidNewRulerClassic=<size=52>Gameplay — Classic Mode</size>\n\n• The game auto-starts once conditions are met.\n\n• At the end of each hand, the main pot winner gains one squid; only one squid can be gained per hand.\n\n• When all squids have been distributed at the end of a hand, the game enters settlement, and all winners split the squid bonus paid by the last player.\n\n• Players who already have a squid may leave early; those without a squid must wait until the game ends before leaving the table.\n\n• If a player force-quits the room, the Squid Game ends immediately, and the leaving player compensates the others. If multiple players leave, the first leaver pays; players who leave simultaneously must all pay.\n\n• New players may join mid-game, and the total number of squids increases accordingly.\n\n
UISquidNewRulerFlight=<size=52>Gameplay — Blood Mode</size>\n\n• The game auto-starts once conditions are met.\n\n• At the end of each hand, the main pot winner receives a squid. Players can obtain multiple squids.\n\n• When the last player has not obtained a squid, or when all squids have been distributed and some players still have not obtained a squid, the game ends; players without a squid pay the compensation.\n\n• New players may join mid-game, and the total number of squids increases accordingly.\n\n
